Result: 在 PubCADBench 和 RealCADBench 基准测试中，VisCAD-M1 实现了最高的平均零件级得分（0.5540），超过了最强的前沿模型（0.5496）。当将 VisCAD-M1 用作测试时验证器时，得分进一步提升至 0.5797，较之前的最先进技术水平提高了约 5%。在装配生成方面，其领域特定工具链在定量和定性评估中均显示出优于通用工具链的优势。

Conclusion: VisCAD 成功提供了一种兼具广泛泛化能力和强大 CAD 专业能力的解决方案。通过在零件级生成上的卓越表现以及通过验证器带来的性能提升，证明了其在处理多样化用户意图方面的有效性。同时，针对装配阶段定制的领域特定方法也证实了结合通用前沿模型与领域专业知识的重要性，为工业产品的 AI 辅助设计提供了新的标杆。

Abstract: AI-assisted computer-aided design (CAD) for industrial products involves two challenging phases. Part-level generation maps diverse forms of user intent, including renders, text descriptions, 2D drawings, and real photographs, to executable programs in a CAD domain-specific language. Assembly-level generation must additionally handle interacting parts, plan mating relations, estimate poses, and place all parts correctly. Existing specialized CAD models are commonly trained on narrow input domains, such as renders or texts, and often generalize poorly, while general-purpose frontier models cover broader inputs but perform inconsistently across CAD domains. We present VisCAD, a foundation model suite designed to provide both broad generalization and strong CAD capability for realistic industrial products. At its core is VisCAD-M1, a 27B model trained through mid-training and post-training for part-level design generation. On PubCADBench and RealCADBench, VisCAD-M1 achieves the highest average part-level score among the evaluated models, reaching 0.5540 compared with 0.5496 for the strongest frontier model. Reusing VisCAD-M1 as a test-time verifier can further raise the score to 0.5797, an approximately 5 percent relative improvement over the previous state of the art. VisCAD also includes a domain-specific harness that leverages frontier models for complex assembly generation and demonstrates advantages over general-purpose harnesses in both quantitative and qualitative evaluations.

</details>


### [71] [SPARK: Input-Conditioned Sparse Activation Modulation for Frozen DiT-based Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03813)
*Federico Putamorsi,Leonardo Zini,Marcella Cornia,Lorenzo Baraldi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SPARK，一种针对冻结的DiT超分辨率模型的轻量级适配方法，通过识别并调整少数主导通道来显著提升图像重建质量，无需微调主干网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的图像超分辨率（SR）模型越来越多地采用扩散Transformer（DiT）骨干网络，其内部激活值往往由少量大规模通道主导。然而，现有的改进感知质量的方法通常需要对网络进行微调或附加额外的适配器，导致这一结构化的激活空间未被充分探索用于模型适配。

Method: 引入SPARK，一个轻量级的输入条件控制器。该方法首先通过在线激活排序过程识别主导通道，然后仅对这些选定的通道预测有界的逐通道仿射变换。整个过程中，SR骨干网络和VAE保持冻结状态，仅优化一个基于低分辨率VAE潜在向量的小型预测器。

Result: 在DIV2K、RealSR和DRealSR三个数据集上，针对三种基于DiT的SR骨干网络进行的实验显示，仅在每个流和块中调制八个通道的情况下，一致提升了保真度和感知质量。对照比较表明，这些增益不能仅由参数预算或对选定通道的访问来解释。

Conclusion: 主导通道可以作为冻结的DiT-based SR模型的紧凑适配接口，SPARK方法通过高效利用这一特性，以极低的计算成本实现了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Real-world image super-resolution (SR) increasingly relies on Diffusion Transformer (DiT) backbones, whose internal activations can be dominated by a small number of massive channels. Yet improving perceptual quality in these models still typically requires fine-tuning the network or attaching additional adapters, leaving this structured activation space largely unexplored for adaptation. We investigate whether dominant channels can instead serve as a compact adaptation interface for frozen DiT-based SR models. We first characterize their behavior in pretrained SR backbones and show through controlled interventions that they strongly affect reconstruction quality. Building on this observation, we introduce SPARK, a lightweight input-conditioned controller that predicts bounded per-channel affine transformations for only the selected channels, while keeping the SR backbone and VAE frozen. Dominant channels are identified through an online activation-ranking procedure, and only a small predictor conditioned on the low-resolution VAE latent is optimized. Experiments on three DiT-based SR backbones across DIV2K, RealSR, and DRealSR show consistent gains in both fidelity and perceptual quality while modulating only eight channels per stream and block. Controlled comparisons further show that these gains cannot be explained by parameter budget or access to the selected channels alone.

</details>


### [72] [Select, Compress, Reinvest: A Controlled Study of Visual-Token Allocation in Long-Video MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03820)
*Prakhar Khatri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究通过控制变量实验证明，在长视频语言模型中，帧选择策略是影响性能的最关键因素。正交匹配追踪（OMP）算法在无训练情况下表现优异，且将压缩节省的预算重新投资到更多帧上能显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有长视频框架比较困难的问题，因为不同研究通常同时改变帧评分器、提示边界、分辨率策略和回答模型等多个变量，导致无法孤立评估单一决策的影响。

Method: 采用控制变量法，固定其他条件，逐一改变帧选择、空间压缩及预算再投资策略。测试了六种无训练选择规则、三个长视频基准和两个回答模型。

Result: 1. 帧选择是最大影响因素：查询选择的8帧比均匀间隔的16帧在LongVideoBench上高出6.9分；未修改的OMP算法性能媲美或接近所有专用选择器。2. 空间压缩成本低：减半空间预算仅造成最多0.44分的损失。3. 预算再投资有效：将节省的token用于处理两倍数量的压缩帧，可额外提升2-3分准确率。

Conclusion: 帧选择策略对长视频理解至关重要，简单的稀疏近似算法（如OMP）即可达到高水平性能。此外，必须在一个受控的实验环境中进行公平比较，以消除实现差异和基准测试工具带来的误差。

Abstract: Long-video language models cannot look at every frame: an hour sampled once per second is 3,600 images, and a system keeps only a small fixed slice of that pool. Which frames survive that slice is usually treated as a preprocessing detail; we test whether it should be. Published selectors make the comparison hard because they change the frame scorer, the prompt boundary, the resolution policy, and the answering model all at once. We hold each fixed and vary one decision at a time: selection, spatial compression, and reinvestment of the savings, across six training-free selection rules, three long-video benchmarks, and two answering models. Selection is the largest single lever: on LongVideoBench's hour-long bin, eight query-selected frames beat sixteen uniformly spaced ones by 6.9 points, and Orthogonal Matching Pursuit, an unmodified decades-old sparse-approximation algorithm, matches or comes within a point of every purpose-built selector we compare it against, across all three benchmarks. Compression is close to free: halving each frame's spatial budget at fixed timestamps costs at most 0.44 points. Reinvestment is where that budget turns back into accuracy: spending the freed tokens on twice as many compressed frames, at a measured cost no higher than the original eight, returns a further two to three points; compression only pays off once its savings are spent this way. Along the way, an implementation bug in our own AKS baseline and a 0.07 to 3.74 point gap between two harnesses running the same published rules at the same budget show why these comparisons need to happen inside one controlled harness rather than across papers.

</details>


### [73] [VI3: Grounding Pretrained 3D Foundation Models with Inertial Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03824)
*Ernesto Lozano,Alberto Jaenal,Javier Civera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VI3是一个模型无关的框架，利用IMU数据为预训练的3D基础模型（3DFM）提供度量尺度锚定，解决单目图像无法获取绝对尺度的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D基础模型在从多视图场景预测相机姿态和稠密深度方面表现出色，具有强大的零样本泛化能力。然而，由于单目图像中不可观测度量尺度，其绝对尺度预测通常不准确。惯性测量单元（IMU）普遍存在于大多数设备中，通过观察缩放运动自然弥补了单目相机的不足。

Method: 提出VI3框架，首先初始化并预积分IMU以获得度量运动参考，然后利用该参考恢复3DFM输出的尺度。该方法包含针对不同3DFM架构的可适应锚定策略，且无需地面真值监督。

Result: 在合成和真实航拍数据集上的实验表明，VI3能够在保持几何一致性的同时恢复度量尺度。在运动条件良好时起到精细细化作用，在运动信息较少时作为强先验使用。

Conclusion: VI3成功证明了仅凭IMU读数即可有效锚定预训练3DFM的度量尺度，提升了其在无监督情况下的尺度准确性及几何一致性。

Abstract: 3D foundation models (3DFMs) excel at predicting camera poses and dense depth from multiple views of a scene, showcasing strong zero-shot generalization. However, as metric scale is not observable from monocular images, their absolute scale predictions are typically inaccurate. Inertial measurement units (IMUs), present in most devices, naturally complement monocular cameras by observing scaled motion. We introduce VI3, a model-agnostic framework that metrically anchors a pretrained 3DFM using only IMU readings. VI3 initializes and preintegrates the IMU to obtain a metric motion reference, which is then used to recover the scale of the 3DFM outputs. Our method includes adaptable anchoring strategies tailored to diverse 3DFM architectures. Experiments on synthetic and real aerial datasets demonstrate that VI3 recovers metric scale without ground-truth supervision while preserving geometric consistency, acting as a fine refinement under well-conditioned motion and as a strong prior when motion is less informative.

</details>


### [74] [The impact of phase information for few-shot fine-grained image classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03829)
*Ruiling Liu,Linyue Zhang,Wenyi Zeng,Jiamiao Lu,Weichuang Zhang,Changming Sun,Zejun Zhang,Xiao Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PSF-Net及API模块，利用相位信息增强少样本细粒度图像分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决少样本细粒度图像分类中特征描述不充分的问题，强调相位信息在捕捉图像结构关系中的关键但未被充分利用的作用。

Method: 引入即插即用的幅度-相位集成（API）模块以结合局部和全局频率的幅度与相位信息；设计PSF-Net网络自适应融合基于相位的空间与频率信息，并支持端到端训练。

Result: 在五个公开数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法优于现有的最先进基准模型。

Conclusion: 有效整合相位信息可显著提升少样本细粒度图像分类任务的表现，且所提方法易于集成到标准训练架构中。

Abstract: Few-shot fine-grained image classification (FSFGIC) aims to classify similar images with limited labeled examples. This work highlights the critical yet underutilized role of phase information in capturing structural relationships within an image. This study introduces a novel plug-and-play amplitude-phase integration (API) module that effectively combines local and global frequency amplitude and phase information for obtaining more comprehensive feature descriptors. Additionally, a dedicated network, named PSF-Net, is proposed that adaptively fuses phase-based spatial and frequency information for FSFGIS. The designed PSF-Net can be easily integrated into standard episodic training architectures for end-to-end training from scratch. Extensive experiments on five public datasets demonstrate that the method outperforms existing state-of-the-art benchmarks.

</details>


### [75] [GraFT: A Training-Free Framework for Spatial Reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models via 3D Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03892)
*Junqing Du,Fernando Ropero,Erkin Turkoz,Yanfeng Zhang,Lu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GraFT，一种无需训练的框架，通过紧凑的3D场景图（3DSG）为多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）提供缺失的3D结构信息，显著提升空间推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MLLMs在3D空间推理中不可靠，难以进行精确几何测量、视角转换及细粒度外观定位；现有解决方案依赖昂贵的监督数据或特定骨干网络，缺乏通用性。

Method: 引入GraFT框架，利用易维护的3DSG提供三种能力：(1) 通过符号工具实现确定性几何；(2) 通过鸟瞰图（BEV）渲染实现全向布局；(3) 通过相关自参考帧实现视觉属性定位。该方法无需训练且解耦于特定骨干网络。

Result: 在ScanQA上，相比同骨干基线各指标均提升，CIDEr提高27%；在VSI-Bench上，冻结状态的MLLMs性能最高提升65%，超越所有专有和开源基线及部分微调模型。

Conclusion: GraFT通过外部结构化知识增强MLLMs的空间推理能力，证明了无需额外训练即可显著弥补模型在3D理解上的缺陷，且优于主流微调方案。

Abstract: 3D spatial reasoning underpins understanding and acting in the physical world, yet it remains unreliable in current multimodal large language models (MLLMs). These models falter at precise geometric measurement, at transforming between egocentric and allocentric viewpoints, and at grounding fine-grained appearance. The most common remedies fine-tune the model on large-scale curated spatial-reasoning datasets or attach dedicated encoders for 3D geometry, which typically couples the solution to costly supervision and a specific backbone. We instead introduce GraFT, a training-free framework that supplies the missing 3D structure through a compact, easily maintained 3D scene graph (3DSG). From this 3DSG, GraFT provides three spatial reasoning capabilities: (1) deterministic geometry through symbolic tools, (2) allocentric layout through a bird's-eye-view (BEV) rendering, and (3) visual-attribute grounding through task-relevant egocentric frames. On ScanQA, GraFT improves every metric over the same-backbone baseline, raising CIDEr by 27%. On VSI-Bench, GraFT improves frozen MLLMs by up to 65%, surpassing every proprietary and general-purpose open-source baseline, and several prominent fine-tuned spatial models.

</details>


### [76] [Concept of a Sensor Test Environment for Dusty Agricultural Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03895)
*Peter Buckel,Johannes Hermann,Jonas Wollmann,Thomas Dietmueller,Timo Oksanen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种包含室内和室外区域的自动驾驶农机扬尘测试场地概念，旨在解决扬尘对传感器和算法性能的干扰问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业中的扬尘严重影响自主农业机械的传感器性能和算法表现，亟需标准化的测试环境来评估和优化系统性能。

Method: 构建一个由室内和室外两部分组成的证明场（proving ground）。室内部分包括封闭循环系统的实验室测试台和可放置全尺寸物体的测试大厅；室外部分配备专用测试装置，支持在真实农业作业中记录有尘和无尘条件下的可重复数据。此外，对测试场地及装置进行了3D可视化展示。

Result: 成功设计并描述了该综合测试场地的架构与功能，实现了在受控环境（室内）和真实环境（室外）下对扬尘影响的对比数据采集能力。

Conclusion: 该测试场地为研究扬尘对自主农机感知系统的影响提供了标准化、可复现的实验平台，有助于提升农机在复杂粉尘环境下的鲁性。

Abstract: Dust in agriculture presents a significant challenge for autonomous agricultural machinery. Dust can impair the performance of sensors and algorithms. This work, therefore, presents a concept for a proving ground consisting of an indoor and outdoor area. The indoor area comprises a laboratory test bench where dust circulates in a closed system and a test hall where life-size objects can be placed. The outdoor area features dedicated test setups that enable reproducible data to be recorded with and without dust during real-world agriculture work. The proving ground and the setups are visualized in 3D.

</details>


### [77] [OctWorld: Long-Range World-Consistent Video Generation with Octree-Based 3D Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03919)
*Zelong Lv,Sicheng Xu,Jianfeng Xiang,Ruicheng Wang,Yue Dong,Yu Deng,Guangzhong Sun,Jiaolong Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OctWorld是一种带有持久化3D记忆的视频扩散框架，通过引入OctMap解决长程生成中的空间一致性问题，实现了高保真、可探索且世界一致的视觉场景生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长程视频生成中（具有延伸的相机路径和宽视角覆盖），当模型重新访问先前生成的区域时，保持空间一致性极具挑战性。现有方法在处理这种长期依赖和全局一致性方面存在不足。

Method: 提出了一种名为OctMap的可扩展、空间自适应的3D记忆机制。该机制利用动态稀疏八叉树进行TSDF融合，根据图像证据自适应调整空间分辨率，逐步将生成的视觉观测及其对应的深度图融合到全局表示中，从而在保留几何与外观细节的同时降低内存开销。

Result: 实验表明，OctWorld能够生成长程、空间一致的高质量视频，在现有基准测试及具有挑战性的长程生成设置上均优于先前方法。此外，OctMap在性能上明显优于基于点的缓存和固定分辨率的TSDF体素方法。

Conclusion: OctWorld通过结合视频扩散模型与自适应3D记忆结构，有效解决了长程视频生成中的空间一致性和内存效率问题，为生成可探索、高保真的3D一致场景提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: We present OctWorld, a video diffusion framework with persistent 3D memory for generating explorable, world-consistent, and high-fidelity visual scenes. Given a single image, OctWorld performs stable autoregressive world generation along user-specified camera trajectories. We focus on long-range generation, characterized by extended camera paths and wide viewpoint coverage, where preserving spatial consistency is particularly challenging when previously generated regions are revisited. To address this problem, we introduce OctMap, an extensible and spatially adaptive 3D memory that progressively fuses generated visual observations and their corresponding depth maps into a global representation. OctMap employs TSDF fusion within a dynamic sparse octree whose spatial resolution adapts to image evidence. This design preserves geometric and appearance details across diverse scene scales while maintaining low memory overhead. Experiments demonstrate that OctWorld generates long-range, spatially consistent videos and outperforms prior methods on both existing benchmarks and challenging long-range generation settings. OctMap also provides clear advantages over point-based caches and fixed-resolution TSDF volumes. Project page: https://maxtirerror.github.io/octworldpage/

</details>


### [78] [Sparse auto-regressive modeling for scene generation from multi-view images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03931)
*Thomas Lucas,Maxime Pietrantoni,Philippe Weinzaepfel,Wonjune Cho,Bardienus Pieter Duisterhof,Vincent Leroy,Jerome Revaud*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPAR3S是一种无需3D真实标签的稀疏体素对齐3D潜在生成模型，通过可微分3D高斯溅射学习稀疏潜在空间，利用掩码自回归Transformer预测缺失体素，实现从稀疏视图到完整3D场景的高效补全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决从稀疏、无约束视图生成完整3D场景的挑战，克服现有前馈重建方法仅能处理可见内容以及3D生成建模因密集体素表示的高计算成本和大规模3D监督数据稀缺而受限的问题。

Method: 提出SPAR3S模型，在结构化且紧凑的稀疏体素对齐3D潜在空间中 formulation 3D场景生成（仅表示占据体素）。使用可微分3D高斯溅射通过光度监督直接从多视图图像学习该稀疏潜在空间。训练一个掩码自回归Transformer，联合建模体素占用和潜在token值，以预测给定部分观测体素后的缺失潜在token及其空间支持。

Result: 在合成室内场景上实现了比先前工作更高的新视角质量；在RealEstate10k数据集上验证了良好的泛化能力，证明了其在真实世界数据中的适用性。

Conclusion: SPAR3S通过结合稀疏潜在空间表示和自回归生成模型，有效解决了无监督条件下的3D场景补全问题，兼具计算效率和高视觉质量，适用于合成及真实场景。

Abstract: Generating complete 3D scenes from sparse, unconstrained views is a fundamental challenge in 3D vision which requires reasoning beyond observed content while remaining computationally tractable. Existing feed-forward reconstruction methods are inherently limited to content visible in the input images, while 3D generative modeling is hindered by the high computational cost of dense volumetric representations and the scarcity of large-scale 3D supervision. We introduce SPAR3S, a sparse voxel-aligned 3D latent generative model for conditional scene completion without requiring ground-truth 3D data for supervision. Our key insight is to formulate 3D scene generation in a structured, compact, voxel-aligned 3D latent space where only occupied voxels are represented. We learn this sparse latent space directly from multi-view images using photometric supervision via differentiable 3D Gaussian Splatting. Given a partial set of observed voxels encoded from sparse input views, scene completion reduces to predicting the missing latent tokens and their spatial support within the voxel grid. To this end, we train a masked autoregressive transformer that jointly models voxel occupancy and latent token values, enabling efficient and spatially consistent generation of unseen regions. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our method on synthetic indoor scenes, achieving higher novel-view quality than prior work. We further validate its generalization on RealEstate10k, highlighting its applicability to real-world data.

</details>


### [79] [WorldReward: Reward Modeling for Camera-Conditioned World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03952)
*Yibin Wang,Zehan Wang,Junshu Tang,Zhimin Li,Yujie Zhou,Jiazi Bu,Pengyang Ling,Feng Han,Zhixiong Zhang,Long Xing,Shengyuan Ding,Ziang Li,Cheng Jin,Yuhang Zang,Jiaqi Wang,Tianyu Pang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WorldReward，一种基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的成对偏好奖励模型，用于评估相机条件世界模型的行动一致性和视觉质量。通过将视频分解为动作对齐的块并聚合决策，解决了长视频上下文噪声问题，并在基准测试中超越GPT-5.5，显著提升了HY-WorldPlay 1.5的强化学习后训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奖励机制存在局限：基于几何的奖励无法评估运动视觉质量，基于图像的奖励无法捕捉动作执行或时间动态。需要一种能统一评估行动一致性与视觉质量的共享推理空间。

Method: 1. 构建WorldReward：将配对视频分解为动作对齐的片段，组织结构化视觉证据，通过投票聚合生成视频级的行动和视觉质量偏好。2. 数据构建：利用前沿VLM生成结构化判断，结合工具代理审计和人工审核，构建大规模推理增强偏好数据集。3. 提出WorldReward-Bench基准进行人类标注评估。

Result: WorldReward在动作一致性、外观质量和运动质量三个维度上均达到最高的人类偏好一致性，分别比GPT-5.5高出3.42、1.45和3.56个百分点。应用于HY-WorldPlay 1.5的RL后训练时，显著改善了从短期到长期的行动执行和视觉质量。

Conclusion: WorldReward通过VLM提供的共享推理空间，有效统一了相机条件世界模型的动作一致性与视觉质量评估，为提升生成视频的交互性和连贯性提供了更优的奖励信号。

Abstract: Camera-conditioned world models generate interactive videos in which commanded actions should induce the expected scene changes while appearance, geometry, and temporal dynamics remain coherent. Existing rewards assess these requirements separately: geometry-based rewards estimate trajectory execution but cannot judge the visual quality of the executed motion, whereas image-based rewards measure frame quality without capturing action execution or temporal dynamics. We posit that a vision-language model (VLM) offers a shared reasoning space for relating actions to their visual outcomes. However, judging a complete long video against its full action sequence creates a lengthy, noisy context in which short-lived local action evidence can be missed or diluted. We present WorldReward, a VLM-based pairwise preference reward model that unifies action-consistency and visual-quality evaluation for camera-conditioned world models. WorldReward decomposes paired videos into action-aligned chunks, organizes each chunk into structured visual evidence, and aggregates chunk-level decisions by voting into separate video-level action and visual-quality preferences. To train it, we construct a large-scale reasoning-augmented preference dataset using structured judgments generated by a frontier VLM and refined through tool-based agent auditing and targeted human review. We further introduce WorldReward-Bench, a human-annotated benchmark measuring reward-model agreement with human preferences across action consistency, appearance quality, and motion quality. WorldReward achieves the highest agreement on all three dimensions, exceeding GPT-5.5 by 3.42, 1.45, and 3.56 percentage points, respectively. When used for RL post-training of HY-WorldPlay 1.5, it consistently improves both action execution and visual quality across short- to long-term horizons.

</details>


### [80] [RARF: Region-Aware Rectified Flows for 3D Brain MRI Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03956)
*Tomas Guija-Valiente,Blanca Rodriguez-Gonzalez,Norberto Malpica,Angel Torrado-Carvajal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RARF框架，用于3D脑MRI修复，通过限制随机插值过程到修复区域并固定观察体素，实现具有解剖一致性的竞争性重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改善自动化脑MRI分析，通过重建病理区域内的健康组织来填补医学图像中的缺失部分。

Method: 引入任务无关的区域感知整流流（Rectified Flow）框架RARF。该方法将随机插值过程限制在修复区域，保持观察体素固定以提供患者特异性解剖上下文。使用三维神经网络接收部分缺失图像、高斯噪声、修复掩码及时间步。训练采用掩码流匹配和重建一致性目标，结合掩码感知的预处理和数据增强。推理时，学习到的速度场将初始噪声传输至合理的缺失组织重建结果。

Result: 在BraTS评估协议下的实验表明，该方法产生了具有竞争力的重建结果，同时保持了解剖一致性。

Conclusion: RARF框架能有效进行3D脑MRI修复，为BraTS Inpainting Challenge 2026提供了有效的解决方案，源代码已公开。

Abstract: Medical image inpainting has the potential to improve automated brain MRI analysis by reconstructing healthy tissue within pathological regions. We introduce RARF, a task-agnostic region-aware rectified flow framework for masked data generation. We instantiate the framework for 3D brain MRI inpainting as our submission to the BraTS Inpainting Challenge 2026. RARF restricts the stochastic interpolation process to the inpainting region, while the observed voxels remain fixed and provide patient-specific anatomical context. A three-dimensional neural network receives the partially voided image, with Gaussian noise filling the missing region, together with the inpainting mask and the corresponding timestep. The model is trained using masked flow-matching and reconstruction-consistency objectives, combined with mask-aware preprocessing and data augmentation. During inference, the learned velocity field transports the initial noise toward a plausible reconstruction of the missing tissue, which is then combined with the unchanged observed anatomy. Experiments under the BraTS evaluation protocol show that the proposed approach produces competitive reconstructions while maintaining anatomical consistency. Source code is available at: https://github.com/TomasGuija/rarf.

</details>


### [81] [Sharpening the Ensemble: An SSIM-Aligned Residual Refiner for Brain-MRI Inpainting Post-Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03981)
*Kubilay Kağan Kömürcü,İlkay Öksüz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过训练轻量级残差细化器对两个顶级模型进行集成后处理，利用加权结构相似度损失，在保持MSE不变的情况下显著提升了BraTS基准测试中的SSIM指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有最强模型在脑MRI修复中因$\ell_1$和MSE损失的均值寻求行为导致的合成区域模糊问题，以便使分析工具能应用于原本会被拒绝的图像。

Method: 构建两个2025年并列第一模型的深度集成，并在其输出上训练一个轻量级残差细化器。损失函数为$\ell_1$损失与结构相似度（SSIM）项的组合，其中SSIM权重$\lambda$经过调整。

Result: 在适度$\lambda$下，SSIM从0.8767提升至0.8780（重现官方评分器），从0.8555提升至0.8572（官方验证排行榜），MSE基本无变化。该改进在62.6%的重现案例中具有统计显著性（p=2.2×10^-7）。消融实验表明，增加第三个模型或传统非锐化掩模均无法达到此效果。

Conclusion: 提出了一种低成本、可复现的后处理阶段，无需大规模重新训练即可显著提升已有强集成模型的性能，证明了增益来源于学习到的优化而非 indiscriminate sharpening（无差别锐化）。

Abstract: Brain-MRI inpainting replaces a masked region of a scan with synthesized, anatomically plausible healthy tissue, so that analysis tools built for healthy brains can be applied to images they would otherwise reject. On the BraTS local-synthesis benchmark, which ranks submissions on the structural similarity index (SSIM), the peak signal-to-noise ratio, and the mean squared error (MSE) jointly, the strongest recent models are accurate, but several report blurry synthesized regions and attribute this to the mean-seeking behavior of the $\ell_1$ and MSE terms in their training losses. We address this in post-processing, forming a deep ensemble of the two co-first-place 2025 models and training a lightweight residual refiner on the ensemble's own outputs under an $\ell_1$ loss augmented with a structural-similarity term whose weight $λ$ we vary. At a moderate $λ$ the refiner improves SSIM over the ensemble, from $0.8767$ to $0.8780$ on a held-out reproduction of the official scorer and from $0.8555$ to $0.8572$ on the official validation leaderboard, with essentially no change in MSE. The gain is small but consistent, improving $62.6\%$ of the held-out cases with a signed-rank $p=2.2\times10^{-7}$, whereas over-weighting the structural term reverses it. Two ablations bound the effect. Adding any third model to the two-model ensemble degrades it, and classical unsharp masking fails to improve SSIM at any strength (best $0.8765$ against $0.8767$), so the gain reflects learned rather than indiscriminate sharpening. The result is a cheap, reproducible post-processing stage that improves an already strong ensemble without any large-scale retraining.

</details>


### [82] [IchthyoNoma: Nomenclature and Context Sensitivity of Zero-Shot Biological Vision--Language Models for Bangladeshi Freshwater Fish Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03985)
*Nazim-E-Alam,Tarek Rahman,Md Kishor Morol*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究审计了零样本视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在孟加拉国淡水鱼类识别中的表现，发现其准确率不仅反映生物学知识，还受多语言对齐、命名法及提示词构建等多重因素影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管零样本VLMs被广泛用于免训练物种识别，但其报告的准确率可能混杂了非视觉因素（如语言偏差），需明确评估其真实能力与局限性。

Method: 对CLIP、BioCLIP、BioCLIP2及Jina CLIP v2进行审计，使用两个孟加拉国来源的7类淡水鱼数据集（共10,321张图像），测试不同语言提示（英语通用名、科学名、孟加拉语）及图像干预（模糊、灰度掩码、白色掩码）下的性能。

Result: BioCLIP2在英语和科学名提示下显著优于通用CLIP（分别达72.36%和68.91% vs 25.15%和14.40%）；但纯孟加拉语提示下性能接近随机水平（约14%），Jina模型仅部分恢复。图像干扰实验显示无显著弱模糊效应，但强模糊/灰度掩码导致适度损失，白色掩码造成较大误差，且结果强烈依赖物种特性。

Conclusion: 零样本生物VLM的得分是生物专业化、多语言对齐、命名规范、提示词设计及上下文环境的综合反映，而非单一视觉知识指标。

Abstract: Zero-shot vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used as training-free species recognizers, but reported accuracy can reflect more than visual species knowledge. We audit CLIP, BioCLIP, BioCLIP2, and a multilingual Jina CLIP v2 control on seven freshwater-fish categories from two Bangladeshi sources (10,321 images). BioCLIP2 reaches 72.36% on BFF-15 with English common names and 68.91% on SylFishBD with scientific names, versus 25.15% and 14.40% for generic CLIP. BioCLIP2 Bengali prompts are near chance in balanced accuracy (14.22-14.29%); Jina partially recovers Bengali discrimination to 21.89% and 16.36%, but bare Bengali names return to 14.29% on both sources. Paired SylFishBD interventions show no significant weak-blur effect, modest losses from stronger blur/gray masking, a larger white-mask artifact, and strong species dependence. Zero-shot biological VLM scores therefore jointly reflect biological specialization, multilingual alignment, nomenclature, prompt formulation, and context.

</details>


### [83] [Catalogue Photography as a Cold Start: Toward Deployable Carbide Burr Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03995)
*Abilash Philip Madavath,Chandra Yuvesh Aubeeluck,Augustin Raju,Nicolas Pyschny,Felix Hackelöer,Florian Zwanzig*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对铣刀和硬质合金旋转锉的质量检测自动化难题，研究利用目录摄影作为唯一监督源在域偏移下的有效性。发现冻结特征提取器效果不佳，度量学习虽在目录图像上聚类效果好但向现场照片迁移率低；通过灰度化和匈牙利分配等简化策略可显著提升迁移性能，证明目录摄影仅适合作为冷启动而非直接训练域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决制造批次中铣刀或硬质合金旋转锉符合生产订单表的验证问题，该任务目前主要依赖人工且易出错。由于缺乏标注图像，计算机视觉自动化面临冷启动约束，制造商目录摄影成为唯一的监督来源，需探究其在域偏移下的适用性。

Method: 1. 评估目录分离性与现场照片性能之间的差距。
2. 测试现成冻结特征提取器对头型和齿形属性的分离能力。
3. 应用度量学习进行无监督聚类发现。
4. 分析模型规模、表示复杂性以及简化策略（如灰度化、基于订单表的匈牙利分配检索约束）对迁移性能的影响。

Result: 1. 现成冻结特征提取器无法可靠分离头型和齿形属性。
2. 度量学习在目录图像上实现近乎完美的无监督聚类（调整兰德指数0.94-0.97），但这一增益在向现场照片迁移时损失超过一半。
3. 最大的迁移增益来自减少域敏感性的简单改变：转换为灰度图（+0.22）和利用已知订单表通过匈牙利分配限制检索（+0.11）。

Conclusion: 目录摄影应被视为有用的冷启动资源，而非可直接用于部署的训练域。研究提供了精密工具制造中目录到现场迁移的经验基线和评估协议。

Abstract: Verifying that manufactured batches of milling tools or carbide rotary burrs conform to production order sheets remains a largely manual and error-prone quality assurance task. Automating this process with computer vision faces a critical cold-start constraint since no labelled imagery is available, leaving manufacturer catalogue photography as the sole source of supervision. We investigate how far catalogue supervision can support an industrial recognition pipeline under domain shift, explicitly measuring the gap between catalogue separability and performance on held-out field photographs. Our findings reveal three key insights. First, off-the-shelf frozen feature extractors do not reliably separate the two task attributes, head shape and tooth profile, motivating targeted representation learning. Second, metric learning produces near-perfect unsupervised cluster discovery on catalogue images (adjusted Rand index 0.94--0.97), but less than half of this gain transfers to field photographs. Third, the largest transfer gains do not come from model scale or representation complexity, but from simple changes that reduce domain sensitivity: converting images to grayscale (+0.22) and constraining retrieval using the known order sheet via Hungarian assignment (+0.11). We therefore treat catalogue photography as a useful cold start rather than a deployment-ready training domain, and provide empirical baselines and an evaluation protocol for catalogue-to-field transfer in precision tool manufacturing.

</details>


### [84] [The Blind Spot in 2D Infants' Pose Estimation:Robust Learning from Noisy Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04009)
*Emanuele Cardinale,Marco Proietti,Alessandro Cacciatore,Maria Francesca Spadea,Lucia Migliorelli,Sara Moccia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出REMIND方法，通过聚类关键点的训练动态来识别姿态估计中的噪声标签，无需预先假设噪声分布。在早产儿临床数据集上验证有效，AUC达93%，解决了数据质量不可靠时的可信学习问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督深度学习依赖高质量标注数据，而噪声标注会严重损害模型性能。尽管分类任务中的标签噪声鲁棒性研究较多，但在姿态估计（PE）中研究不足。特别是在新生儿学等临床场景中，早产儿图像标注因自遮挡、看护人干扰等视觉挑战易出错，且PE用于评估自发运动以预测神经发育轨迹，因此解决此问题至关重要。

Method: 引入REliable keypoint selection via Memory of traINing Dynamics (REMIND)。这是一种基于聚类的关键点选择策略，利用关键点级别的训练动态来识别噪声标签。该方法不假设任何关于噪声分布的先验知识，从而实现无噪声模型的训练。

Result: 在包含46名早产儿真实临床场景视频的NeoPose专有数据集上进行了评估。REMIND在不同噪声情况下正确识别了噪声标注，在使用相关文献中的三种不同PE架构时，达到了高达93%的曲线下面积（AUC）。

Conclusion: 这是首个明确解决早产儿姿态估计中标签噪声问题的研究。该工作为在数据质量无法保证的情况下，设计用于婴儿监测支持的可信基于学习的算法铺平了道路。

Abstract: Noisy annotations pose a significant challenge for supervised deep learning, as neural networks rely on large-scale, high-quality labeled data whose corruption can severely impair model performance. Although robustness to label noise has been extensively studied for classification tasks, it remains relatively underexplored in Pose Estimation (PE). This limitation becomes critical in clinical contexts, including neonatology, where PE of preterm infants is used to support the assessment of spontaneous motility, a key indicator of neurodevelopmental trajectories. In such settings, infants' images labeling is further hindered by visual challenges (e.g., keypoint self-occlusions, caregiver interference), making the annotation process inherently susceptible to errors. To tackle noisy annotations in PE, we introduce REliable keypoint selection via Memory of traINing Dynamics (REMIND), a clustering-based keypoint-selection strategy that exploits keypoint-wise training dynamics to identify noisy labels without assuming any prior knowledge of the noise distribution, thus enabling noise-free model training. When evaluated on the proprietary NeoPose dataset, comprising 46 videos of 46 preterm infants recorded in real clinical settings, REMIND correctly identifies noisy annotations across multiple corruption scenarios, achieving up to 93\% Area Under the Curve (AUC) with three different PE architectures used in the relevant literature. To our knowledge, this is the first study to explicitly address label noise in preterm infants' PE, paving the way for the design of trustworthy learning-based algorithms for infants'monitoring support when data quality cannot be guaranteed.

</details>


### [85] [Stable and Scalable Bundle Adjustment of Holistic 3D Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04026)
*Shaohui Liu,Rémi Pautrat,Daniel Barath,Richard Hartley,Viktor Larsson,Marc Pollefeys*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一的束调整（BA）框架，通过将高阶几何关系（如共面、平行）建模为类似相机的实体，并利用2D重投影误差进行优化，实现了在保持稀疏性和计算效率的同时，联合优化几何特征与高阶结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的束调整主要优化相机内参、位姿和稀疏3D点。虽然已有扩展涉及线条等基元，但整合更丰富的几何结构（如平行性、共面性或线框）通常会导致计算成本显著增加和数值稳定性降低。现有方法难以在保持高效性的同时处理这些高阶关系。

Method: 1. 构建分类体系：区分具有直接2D测量的可扩展几何特征（点、线）和编码高阶关系的组（共面、平行等），将“组”建模为BA框架内的类相机实体。
2. 统一表达：将组约束和跨特征关系（如点-线关联）表示为2D重投影测量。
3. 误差 formulation：构建由组诱导的和跨特征的重投影误差，避免直接使用会降低条件数和稳定性的3D正则化。
4. 保留稀疏性：通过舒尔补消去法（Schur elimination）保留经典基于点的BA的稀疏结构。

Result: 在真实世界和合成数据集上的实验表明，该方法的运行时间与仅基于点的经典束调整相当，但能生成更丰富的3D结构，并提高了几何精度。

Conclusion: 提出的统一框架成功地将高阶几何关系集成到束调整中，在不牺牲计算效率和数值稳定性的前提下，显著提升了3D重建的结构丰富度和几何准确性。

Abstract: Bundle Adjustment (BA) is a cornerstone of 3D computer vision and has benefited from decades of advances in sparse optimization and numerical methods. It was originally developed for jointly optimizing camera intrinsics, poses and sparse 3D points. While extensions incorporate lines and other primitives, integrating richer geometric structures such as parallelism, coplanarity, or wireframes often introduces significantly increased computational cost and reduced numerical stability. In this paper, we propose a unified framework that extends bundle adjustment to jointly optimize geometric features and higher-order relations. We first introduce a taxonomy that distinguishes scalable geometric features with direct 2D measurements (e.g., points and lines), from groups encoding higher-order relations (e.g., coplanarity, parallelism, etc.), where we show that groups can be modeled as camera-like entities within the bundle adjustment framework. Building on this formulation, we propose that both group constraints and cross-feature relations (i.e., point-line associations) can be expressed through 2D reprojection measurements. By formulating group-induced and cross-feature reprojection errors, we preserve the sparsity structure of classical point-based BA under Schur elimination, while avoiding direct 3D regularization that degrades the conditioning and stability. Experiments on both real-world and synthetic datasets demonstrate runtime performance comparable to classical point-only bundle adjustment, while producing significantly richer 3D structures and improved geometric accuracy.

</details>


### [86] [DSAQuant: Denoising-Stage-Aligned Quantization-Aware Training for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04031)
*Shuaiting Li,Zelin Gao,Haibin Shen,Yujun Shen,Haotong Qin,Yinghao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DSAQuant框架，通过去噪阶段对齐的量化感知训练和推理引导，解决视频扩散模型量化后细节丢失问题，显著提升W4A4/W3A3设置下的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型（VDMs）因高计算成本难以部署，现有量化感知训练（QAT）方法虽保留语义和布局，但严重损害视觉细节、纹理保真度和清晰度，原因是传统量化设计忽略了去噪的阶段性功能差异。

Method: 提出DSAQuant框架：1. 训练阶段采用“去噪阶段导向监督”，早期步骤保持教师蒸馏以稳定结构，后期步骤转向目标驱动优化以增强细节重建；2. 推理阶段采用“去噪阶段门控引导”，在最终去噪步骤禁用CFG，防止量化误差被放大为高频伪影。

Result: 在Wan和CogVideoX系列模型上进行W4A4和W3A3量化实验，DSAQuant consistently超越SOTA基线，VBench平均分提升高达6.60，同时保持强大的文本-视频对齐能力。

Conclusion: 有效的VDM量化需将训练与推理过程与视频扩散的去噪阶段性特征对齐，而非仅减少量化误差。

Abstract: Video diffusion models (VDMs) have achieved impressive progress in text-to-video generation, but their high memory and computational costs hinder practical deployment. Quantization-aware training (QAT) is an effective solution for compressing and accelerating advanced generative models without runtime overhead at inference. However, existing QAT methods suffer from a distinctive challenge in VDMs: while they often preserve prompt semantics, global layout, and coarse motion, the quantized model severely degrades visual details, texture fidelity, and sharpness. In this paper, we trace this degradation to the timestep-agnostic design of conventional quantization pipelines, which overlooks the stage-wise functionality of video denoising. In VDMs, early denoising steps mainly establish global structure and motion, whereas middle and late steps refine local appearance and high-frequency details. Based on this insight, we propose DSAQuant, a Denoising-Stage-Aligned Quantization-aware training framework for VDMs. During training, Denoising-Stage Oriented Supervision preserves teacher distillation in early steps for stable structure planning, while shifting later steps toward target-driven optimization to enhance detail reconstruction. During inference, Denoising-Stage Gated Guidance disables CFG in the final denoising steps to prevent it from amplifying quantization-induced errors into high-frequency artifacts. Extensive experiments on the Wan and CogVideoX families under W4A4 and W3A3 settings show that DSAQuant consistently outperforms the SOTA QAT baseline, improving the VBench average score by up to 6.60 under aggressive W3A3 quantization while preserving strong text-video alignment. These results demonstrate that effective VDM quantization requires not only reducing quantization error, but also aligning quantization training and inference with the stage-wise nature of video diffusion.

</details>


### [87] [Editable Visual Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04034)
*Junyan Ye,Wei Liu,Dongzhi Jiang,Zichen Wen,HaoDong Li,Zhutao Lv,Jiaxin Lin,Jinhua Yu,Jun He,Zilong Huang,Rui Chen,Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出“可编辑视觉设计”新范式，结合VLM的创意规划与图像生成模型的资产合成，通过编码代理实现具有解耦图层和真实文本的高质量、可编辑视觉内容生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决基于扩散模型（如GPT-Image-2）生成的扁平化位图缺乏图层控制且文字易出错的问题，同时克服纯代码生成方法缺乏全局审美直觉及复杂资产构建困难的局限。

Method: 提出由编码代理驱动的“可编辑视觉设计”框架：利用视觉语言模型（VLM）作为“创意大脑”负责需求理解、任务规划和审美判断；将图像生成模型作为按需“视觉世界模拟器”合成独立视觉资产；采用“先想象后行动”的闭环工作流，生成孤立资产并编写原生HTML/CSS，根据视觉渲染反馈迭代优化设计。

Result: 系统成功生成了具有解耦图层和真实文本的可编辑 artifacts，支持用户在图形界面中进行直观的鼠标拖拽和布局调整。在海报、信息图等场景的验证表明，该范式兼具精致的审美效果和生产级的可编辑性。此外，“Agent Design Replay”功能还忠实复现了类似专业人类设计师的创作与推理轨迹。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种新的视觉设计范式，通过整合VLM的高层认知能力与代码生成的精确控制力，实现了既美观又易于后期编辑的视觉内容生产，弥合了自动化生成与人工精细控制之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: While diffusion base models such as GPT-Image-2 and Nano-Banana exhibit remarkable visual expressiveness, their end-to-end generation inherently yields flattened bitmaps with error-prone text, precluding layer-wise post-editing. Conversely, code-based visual generation via Coding Agents provides precise layout control and decoupled layers, yet remains constrained by a lack of global aesthetic intuition and the difficulty of coding complex visual assets.
  To address this, we propose Editable Visual Design, a new paradigm driven by a Coding Agent. We designate the VLM as the ``creative brain'' for requirement comprehension, task planning, and aesthetic judgment, while utilizing the image generation model as an on-demand ``visual world simulator'' to synthesize standalone visual assets. Operating under an ``imagine first, then act'' closed-loop workflow, the agent generates isolated assets, writes native HTML/CSS, and iteratively refines the design against visual rendering feedback.
  Furthermore, Agent Design Replay faithfully reproduces the creative and reasoning trajectory akin to that of professional human designers. Ultimately, the system delivers editable artifacts with decoupled layers and real text, enabling users to perform intuitive mouse dragging and layout adjustments on a graphical user interface. Validations on posters, infographics, and other scenarios show that this paradigm successfully achieves both refined aesthetics and production-grade editability.

</details>


### [88] [Continuous Actions from Discrete Minds: Latent-Aligned Planning for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04070)
*Ruoyu Yao,Yusen Xie,Qingzhao Liu,Pei Liu,Zewei Yang,Yipeng Zhu,Xiaolong Wang,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LaPla框架，通过连续潜在空间对齐解决VLA模型在自动驾驶中离散推理与连续物理约束间的差距，消除量化误差并提升规划效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合视觉-语言模型（VLM）的离散推理能力与自动驾驶所需的连续、受物理约束的运动执行之间的鸿沟，克服传统基于码本查找方法带来的量化误差及自回归生成的低效问题。

Method: 构建统一视觉-语言-动作（VLA）框架LaPla，采用基于残差向量量化变分自编码器（VQ-VAE）的动作分词器；利用并发动作查询在单次前向传播中将隐藏状态直接投影至预训练VQ-VAE的连续潜在空间，并通过冻结解码器将连续潜在变量转化为动作，实现语义到运动的无缝接地。

Result: 在nuScenes基准测试中，长视界L2误差较最先进VLA方法降低15.52%；在NVIDIA AlpaSim模拟器闭环评估中，成功率提升33.34个百分点，且显著降低了推理延迟。

Conclusion: LaPla通过连续潜在表示有效消除了量化误差，确保了物理上合理的轨迹生成，同时避免了耗时的自回归过程，展现了优异的开放环性能和闭环驾驶平滑性。

Abstract: Bridging the gap between the discrete reasoning of Vision-Language Models and the continuous, physics-constrained nature of autonomous driving remains a significant challenge. In this work, we introduce LaPla, a unified Vision-Language-Action (VLA) framework featuring latent-aligned planning to seamlessly ground semantic understanding in precise motion execution. We first design an action tokenizer based on a residual vector-quantized variational autoencoder (VQ-VAE), capturing vehicle kinematics and encoding trajectory features into a structured latent space. Rather than discrete codebook lookups that inevitably introduce quantization errors, LaPla repurposes this representation as a physical prior to bridge the modality gap between high-dimensional semantics and the raw action space. Specifically, given multimodal inputs integrating multi-view images, historical actions, and textual instructions, LaPla incorporates concurrent action queries to causally attend to the multimodal context in a single forward pass, projecting hidden states directly into the pretrained VQ-VAE latent space. The frozen decoder then translates these continuous latents into actions, effectively eliminating quantization errors and ensuring physically plausible trajectories while bypassing time-consuming autoregressive generation. Extensive experiments on the nuScenes benchmark demonstrate that LaPla achieves competitive open-loop performance, reducing long-horizon L2 error by 15.52% compared to state-of-the-art VLA methods. Closed-loop evaluations on the NVIDIA AlpaSim simulator further confirm its superior capability in ensuring smooth driving progress, improving the success rate by 33.34 percentage points with significantly reduced inference latency.

</details>


### [89] [TAP-Path: Task-Adaptive Structural and Token Pruning for Efficient and Trustworthy Pathology Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04071)
*Mehedi Hasan,Ashfak Yeafi,Md Khairul Islam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TAP-Path框架，通过任务自适应压缩重构Virchow2编码器，在显著降低参数量和计算成本的同时，保持了甚至提升了病理学基础模型的分类精度和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有病理学基础模型（如数百兆参数的编码器）推理成本高、效率低的问题，寻求在保持高性能的前提下优化准确率与效率的权衡。

Method: 提出TAP-Path任务自适应压缩框架，直接重构预训练的Virchow2编码器而非知识蒸馏。核心方法包括：验证驱动的Transformer块选择、冗余块的物理移除、输入自适应的patch-token剪枝、多深度特征恢复以及轻量级门控任务头。

Result: 模型保留24/32个Transformer块和70%的patch token，参数量减少24.96%（631.24M至473.70M），计算量减少35.20%。在32类基准测试中，准确率达87.98%，优于完整Virchow2（86.89%）和UNI2-h（87.67%）。外部CPTAC评估显示准确率为91.22%，证明了其在内部和外部评估中的可靠性及稀有类别识别能力的提升。

Conclusion: 任务自适应的结构和Token稀疏化能够改善大型病理学基础模型的准确率-效率权衡，同时在不损失可靠性的前提下实现模型压缩和优化。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models improve transferable representation learning for histopathology, but recent gains often rely on encoders with hundreds of millions of parameters and high inference cost. We propose TAP-Path, a task-adaptive compression framework that directly restructures a pretrained Virchow2 encoder rather than distilling it into a separate student. TAP-Path combines validation-driven transformer-block selection, physical removal of redundant blocks, input-adaptive patch-token pruning, multi-depth feature recovery, and a lightweight gated task head. The final model retains 24 of 32 transformer blocks and 70% of patch tokens after pruning, reducing encoder parameters by 24.96% (631.24M to 473.70M) and analytical encoder compute by 35.20% (340.13G to 220.40G FLOPs). Across three task-head optimization seeds, TAP-Path achieved $87.98 \pm 0.067%$ test accuracy, $81.26 \pm 0.49%$ balanced accuracy, and $82.38 \pm 0.48%$ macro-F1 on a 32-class histopathology benchmark, compared with 86.89% for full Virchow2 and 87.67% for UNI2-h. TAP-Path achieved a Brier score of $0.1800 \pm 0.0005$ and failure-detection AUROC of $0.9047 \pm 0.0060$. A validation-only rare-aware objective improved rare-class balanced accuracy in a secondary operating analysis. Frozen external evaluation on 433 CPTAC samples yielded $91.22 \pm 0.83%$ accuracy and $91.10 \pm 0.81%$ balanced accuracy. These results show that task-adaptive structural and token sparsification can improve the accuracy-efficiency trade-off of large pathology foundation models while preserving reliability under internal and external evaluation.

</details>


### [90] [Efficient Semantic Understanding from Digital Foveation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04088)
*Caterina Caccavella,Vittorio Fra,Andreas Ziegler,Giulia D'Angelo,Yulia Sandamirskaya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于数字中央凹视觉的高效语义理解框架，通过选择性计算和稀疏观测，以极低算力成本实现接近基线的物体级识别精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统密集语义分割对整张图像均匀分配计算资源，忽视了场景复杂度和任务相关性，导致计算效率低下；受生物视觉启发，旨在探索更高效的语义理解方式。

Method: 引入轻量级主动视觉流水线，结合显著性驱动的注视点选择、高分辨率中央凹观测、低分辨率上下文信息、语义累积及自适应计算机制，并采用物体级评估而非传统的像素级指标。

Result: 在ADE20K-Object数据集上，单次中央凹观测仅需4.7%的计算成本即可达到基线Top-1准确率95.9%和Top-3准确率96.9%；在场景层面，语义累积仅用58.6%的计算量即恢复90.6%的基线物体召回率。

Conclusion: 当计算资源被选择性分配时，稀疏观测足以产生显著的语义理解；主动视觉是均匀密集处理的高效替代方案，且需超越传统像素级分割指标建立新的评估协议。

Abstract: Dense semantic segmentation allocates computational resources uniformly across the entire image, regardless of scene complexity or task relevance. Inspired by biological vision, we investigate whether semantic understanding can be achieved more efficiently through digital foveated perception. We introduce a lightweight active-vision pipeline that combines saliency-driven fixation selection, high-resolution foveal observations, low-resolution contextual information, semantic accumulation, and adaptive computation. Beyond conventional dense prediction metrics, we use object-level evaluation to measure semantic understanding under sparse observations. On ADE20K-Object, a single foveated observation achieves 95.9% of the baseline Top-1 accuracy and 96.9% of the baseline Top-3 accuracy while requiring only 4.7% of the computational cost. At the scene level, semantic accumulation recovers 90.6% of the baseline object recall while using 58.6% of the computation. These results suggest that substantial semantic understanding can emerge from sparse observations when computation is allocated selectively, highlighting active vision as an efficient alternative to uniform dense processing and motivating evaluation protocols beyond conventional pixel-wise segmentation metrics.

</details>


### [91] [The Shape of Time: Video-Token Contrast for Temporal Understanding in VideoLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04110)
*Yumeng Shi,Quanyu Long,Yin Wu,Wenya Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VT-Contrast，一种针对VideoLMs的表示层时间反事实目标，通过在晚期层对视频token进行时序对比监督，解决模型依赖捷径而非真正理解时间动态的问题，无需修改架构即可提升时序理解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视频语言模型（VideoLMs）虽然接收有序视频流，但其主要监督信号作用于生成的文本而非视频token表示，导致模型可能通过物体、场景或语言先验等捷径学习时序答案，而未能内部捕捉事件进展。

Method: 提出VT-Contrast方法，对选定的晚期层最后一帧视频token进行监督，构建保持顺序的视图与根据Kendall tau距离排序的重排反事实视图进行对比，以暴露时间差异。

Result: 该方法无需改变模型架构，兼容多种VideoLM训练任务，并在多个时序理解基准测试中提升了整体性能。

Conclusion: 通过在视频表示层面引入时序反事实对比学习，能有效促使模型捕捉事件进展，改善VideoLMs的时序理解能力。

Abstract: Seeing frames in order does not mean representing time. Modern VideoLMs receive ordered video streams, yet their main supervision acts on generated text rather than video-token representations where event dynamics should first emerge. This mismatch allows models to learn temporal answers from shortcuts such as objects, scenes, and language priors, without requiring internal video representations to capture event progression. To address this, we propose VT-Contrast, a representation-level temporal counterfactual objective for VideoLMs. Its design asks where temporal supervision should act and what temporal differences it should expose. VT-Contrast supervises selected late-layer last-frame video tokens, where temporal information is expected to be integrated before language generation, and contrasts order-preserving views with same-video reordered counterfactuals graded by Kendall tau distance. It requires no architectural changes, is compatible with diverse VideoLM training tasks, and improves overall performance across temporal understanding benchmarks. Our code is available at https://github.com/ANDgate99/VT-Contrast.

</details>


### [92] [BooM-VVT: Boosting Mask-Free Video Virtual Try-On with Image-Level Pseudo Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04120)
*Wei Zhang,Xin Li,Peishu Shi,Jialin Gao,Xuekang Peng,Zhichao Lian,Yeying Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BooM-VVT，一种基于关键帧驱动的无掩码视频虚拟试穿框架。通过多阶段训练利用图像级伪数据实现无掩码定位，引入对服装敏感的关键帧采样和Frame-Shared 3D-RoPE提升一致性，并构建OmniView数据集以支持复杂视角下的生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于关键帧的视频虚拟试穿方法依赖掩码定位，易受大幅运动和严重遮挡影响；而无掩码图像试穿方法难以扩展至视频，因视频级伪数据构建成本过高。此外，粗糙的关键帧采样和多视角试穿数据的稀缺限制了服装一致性和任务多样性。

Method: 1. 多阶段训练策略：利用图像级伪数据进行无掩码定位学习，降低对视频级伪数据的依赖。2. 对服装敏感的关键帧采样（Garment-Sensitive Keyframe Sampling）：基于与服装相关的身体区域选择关键帧，以更好捕捉服装外观。3. Frame-Shared 3D-RoPE：建立关键帧与目标视频帧之间的时空对应关系，实现准确的服装细节迁移。4. 构建OmniView大规模多视角试穿数据集。

Result: 大量实验表明，BooM-VVT在时间一致性和服装保真度方面优于现有方法。

Conclusion: BooM-VVT成功解决了视频虚拟试穿中掩码依赖、数据成本高及一致性差的问题，实现了更鲁棒、高质量的无掩码视频试穿生成。

Abstract: Video virtual try-on (VVT) aims to generate realistic videos of a person wearing a target garment. Recent methods leverage a keyframe-driven video generation paradigm to improve in-the-wild performance, yet they still rely on masks to localize try-on regions, making them vulnerable to large motions and severe occlusions. Although mask-free image-based try-on methods have shown promising results by leveraging large-scale pseudo data, extending this paradigm to videos remains difficult, as constructing video-level pseudo data is prohibitively expensive. Furthermore, coarse keyframe sampling and the scarcity of multi-view try-on data limit existing keyframe-driven methods in maintaining garment consistency and handling diverse try-on tasks.
  To address these challenges, we propose BooM-VVT, a mask-free VVT framework built upon the keyframe-driven paradigm. To achieve mask-free VVT, we introduce a multi-stage training strategy that leverages image-level pseudo data for mask-free localization learning, substantially reducing the need for costly video-level pseudo data. To improve garment consistency, we propose Garment-Sensitive Keyframe Sampling, which selects keyframes based on garment-relevant body regions to better capture garment appearance. We further introduce Frame-Shared 3D-RoPE to establish spatiotemporal correspondences between keyframes and target video frames for accurate garment-detail transfer. Finally, we construct OmniView, a large-scale multi-view try-on dataset to support reliable try-on video generation under complex camera viewpoints and diverse try-on tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BooM-VVT achieves superior temporal consistency and garment fidelity over existing methods. Project page: https://boomvvt.github.io/boomvvt.

</details>


### [93] [Beyond Retrieval: Progressive Latent Memory Evolution for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04131)
*Hongyu Qu,Guangming Yao,Ling Xing,Xiaobin Hu,Rongxing Ding,Guibin Zhang,Fan Zhang,Yi Yuan,Xiangbo Shu,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LatentStream框架，通过“检索-内化”机制将流式视频历史证据转化为紧凑的潜在工作记忆，取代传统的“存储-检索”范式，在严格因果性和有限内存约束下实现更高效的流式视频理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法采用“存储-检索”范式，将历史观察压缩为外部记忆库并检索相关证据作为额外视觉上下文。这种方式导致历史证据无法内化为紧凑、持续演进的潜在记忆，从而限制了其对流式推理过程的持续指导能力。

Method: 引入LatentStream框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 查询无关的分层流式记忆（Query-agnostic Hierarchical Streaming Memory），通过Jenks引导的自适应整合，在固定预算下将视觉历史组织为短、中、长期层级；2) 分层潜在记忆演化（Hierarchical Latent Memory Evolution），赋予潜在记忆token逐步扩展的感受野，使其能迭代检索对应范围内的历史证据并将其内化为固定长度的紧凑潜在记忆；3) 渐进式置信度引导的潜在记忆优化（Progressive Confidence-guided Latent Memory Optimization），基于组级预测熵构建分层递进奖励，联合优化潜在记忆token和检索到的证据，以鼓励更具信心的流式推理。

Result: 在现有的在线和离线视频基准测试中，LatentStream取得了新的最先进（SOTA）结果。

Conclusion: LatentStream通过将从“存储-检索”转向“检索-内化”的渐进式潜在工作记忆机制，有效解决了流式视频理解中历史证据难以持续指导推理的问题，显著提升了模型性能。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to process continuous visual inputs and respond to user queries under strict causality and bounded memory. Existing approaches typically compress historical observations into an external memory bank and retrieve query-relevant evidence as additional visual context. Though effective, this store-and-retrieve paradigm keeps historical evidence as external visual context, preventing it from being internalized into a compact, evolving latent memory that can continuously guide streaming reasoning. To bridge this gap, we introduce LatentStream, a progressive latent working memory framework that shifts streaming memory from store-and-retrieve to retrieve-and-internalize. Specifically, LatentStream comprises three coordinated components. First, Query-agnostic Hierarchical Streaming Memory organizes visual history into short-, mid-, and long-term levels under a fixed memory budget through Jenks-guided adaptive consolidation. Once a query arrives, Hierarchical Latent Memory Evolution equips groups of latent memory tokens with progressively expanding memory receptive fields, enabling them to iteratively retrieve historical evidence from their corresponding scopes and internalize it into a compact, fixed-length latent memory. Finally, Progressive Confidence-guided Latent Memory Optimization constructs a hierarchical progression reward from group-wise predictive entropy and jointly refines the latent memory tokens and retrieved evidence, encouraging increasingly confident streaming reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LatentStream achieves new state-of-the-art results on existing online and offline video benchmarks.

</details>


### [94] [Persistent Identity Preservation in Generative Image Models: A Benchmark and Evaluation System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04151)
*Mengwei Ren,Xuaner Zhang,Zhihao Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究对比了现有主体驱动图像生成方法在身份保持上的差异，发现持久化身份表示能显著减少迭代编辑和复杂场景下的身份退化，证明身份保持需独立建模而非仅依赖模型能力提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决生成式图像模型在生成或编辑特定主体时出现的“身份漂移”问题，即随着姿态、表情、视角等变化，主体身份特征难以保持。

Method: 系统性地对三种主流范式进行基准测试：基于输入上下文的方法（如GPT-Image-2, NB2）、可训练的主体特定模型参数（如LoRA）以及跨生成和编辑重用的持久化身份层（如PHOTA IDENTITY）。任务涵盖主体驱动生成、编辑、修复及多主体场景，旨在逐步增加对身份保持的压力。

Result: 1. 身份保持仍是当前基础模型的显著局限，高图像质量和指令遵循并不保证身份保真度；2. 在迭代编辑、小尺度主体、严重图像退化和多主体合成中，身份退化加剧；3. 持久化身份表示在这些场景下显著减少了身份退化，在不同基础模型上均提升了身份保持能力，同时保持了相当的指令遵循度和感知图像质量。

Conclusion: 身份保持并非单纯由生成模型能力的提升而自然涌现，而是可以作为一种独立的持久化主体知识进行表示，并与底层生成模型组合使用，从而有效解决身份漂移问题。

Abstract: Generative image models can now produce high-quality images, follow complex instructions, and support precise edits, but they still struggle to preserve who or what is being depicted. When generating or editing images of a specific subject, identity may drift as the pose, expression, appearance, viewpoint, or surrounding scene changes. Existing subject-driven methods make fundamentally different choices about where identity is represented: through the input context (GPT-Image-2, NB2), as trainable subject-specific model parameters (LoRA), or as a persistent identity layer (PHOTA IDENTITY) reusable across generations and edits. We systematically benchmark these paradigms across subject-driven generation, editing, restoration, and multi-subject settings, with tasks designed to increasingly stress identity preservation. Our results show that identity preservation remains a distinct limitation of current generative foundation models: strong image quality and instruction following do not necessarily imply strong identity fidelity, and identity degradation becomes more pronounced under iterative edits, small subject scales, severe image degradation, and multi-subject composition. Persistent identity substantially reduces this degradation across generation, editing, and restoration, consistently improving identity preservation when applied to different foundation models while maintaining comparable instruction adherence and perceptual image quality. These results suggest that identity does not simply emerge from increasingly capable generative models, but can instead be represented as persistent subject knowledge that is composed independently with the underlying generative model.

</details>


### [95] [Zero-Shot Novel Depth Synthesis Using 3D Foundation Models Scene Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04174)
*Denis M. Akola,David F. Fouhey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Z3D方法，利用3D基础模型（如VGGT）的内部表征进行潜在扩散，从而在新视角下预测逼真的深度图（点图），实现无需重新训练的高效3D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D基础模型（3DFMs）已展现出强大的3D视觉能力，但其内部表征是否蕴含可直接用于新视角3D重建的几何信息尚不明确。旨在验证能否从这些模型的隐藏层中解码出表面信息，并利用其学到的通用3D场景知识进行推理。

Method: 提出Z3D方法，通过在3DFM的特征表示上进行潜在扩散（latent diffusion），直接估计未见视角的点图（pointmaps）。该方法利用了3DFM编码器中提取的丰富语义和几何隐含信息。

Result: 证明了可以从3DFM的内部表征中解码隐藏表面；Z3D能够在多个数据集上为新视角预测出逼真的深度图，展示了其在3D重建任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 3D基础模型的内部表征包含了丰富的3D场景通用知识，通过适当的解码机制（如潜在扩散），可以高效地将其应用于新视角的深度估计和3D重建任务。

Abstract: 3D Foundation Models (3DFMs) such as VGGT have recently pushed the boundaries of 3D vision by predicting rich unified representations with feed-foward transformers. The scene representations learned by these models enable strong performance on multiple 3D vision tasks. In this paper, we investigate using their internal representations to infer 3D in the scene from new views. Our hypothesis is that in order to solve the task of 3D reconstruction, these models need to learn a representation that includes a large amount of general knowledge about 3D scenes. After showing that it is possible to decode hidden surfaces from internal 3DFM representations, we propose a method, Z3D, that estimates pointmaps in unseen views by doing latent diffusion on 3DFM representation. We show that Z3D can predict realistic depth maps for new views across multiple datasets.

</details>


### [96] [Seeing Before Synthesizing: VLM-Guided Transition Event Discovery for Weakly-Supervised Dense Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04183)
*Ye-Chan Kim,Seunghee Choi,SeungJu Cha,Si-Woo Kim,Hwiseon Kim,Hyungee Kim,Dong-Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SBS框架，利用VLM生成帧级叙事以自适应地定位视频事件转换点，解决现有LLM辅助方法缺乏视觉 grounding 和固定分配的问题，在ActivityNet Captions和YouCook2数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的弱监督密集视频描述方法中，利用大语言模型（LLM）合成的辅助过渡字幕缺乏视觉 grounding，且被僵化地分配给固定的位置和时长，无法灵活适应实际视频内容。

Method: 提出“先观察后合成”（Seeing Before Synthesizing, SBS）框架：1. 利用视觉-语言模型（VLM）为事件间隙生成帧级叙事；2. 通过检测叙事间的语义变化来识别转换点；3. 结合时间中点和语义变化点，并选择最大化视觉-语言对齐度的宽度，从而细化事件间隙的时间掩码。

Result: 在ActivityNet Captions和YouCook2两个基准数据集上进行了实验，证明了该方法在视频描述（captioning）和事件定位（localization）方面均达到了最先进的性能（State-of-the-art）。

Conclusion: SBS框架通过自适应地提供具有视觉 grounding 的语言指导，有效解决了传统方法中过渡字幕分配僵化的问题，显著提升了弱监督密集视频描述的任务效果。

Abstract: Weakly-Supervised Dense Video Captioning aims to localize and describe multiple events in untrimmed videos given only an ordered set of event-level captions per video. Recent work synthesizes auxiliary transition captions via LLM to provide additional vision-language alignment, but these captions lack visual grounding and are rigidly assigned to every inter-event gap at a fixed location and duration. To address these, we propose Seeing Before Synthesizing (SBS), a framework that adaptively provides visually grounded linguistic guidance only where warranted. Leveraging a VLM, we generate frame-level narratives for the inter-event gaps and detect transitions from the semantic variation across them. For identified transitions, we then refine inter-event temporal masks by blending the temporal midpoint with the semantic change point and selecting the width that maximizes vision-language alignment. Experiments on ActivityNet Captions and YouCook2 demonstrate state-of-the-art performance in both captioning and localization.

</details>


### [97] [One Editor, Many Edits: A Unified Training-Free Framework for Diverse Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04190)
*Adheesh Sunil Juvekar,Onkar Kishor Susladkar,Kiet A. Nguyen,Muntasir Wahed,Nabeel Bashir,Xiaona Zhou,Tianjiao Yu,Vedant Shah,Ismini Lourentzou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EditVid，一种无需训练的 unified 视频编辑框架，结合稀疏因果记忆、基于对应关系的后注意力令牌注入和软潜在混合技术，同时支持指令引导和参考引导编辑，在FiVE基准测试中取得78.16的准确率，显著优于基线，并在用户研究中获得51.8%的偏好率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决单一统一框架内实现高质量指令引导和主体引导视频编辑的挑战，克服现有方法在局部连贯性、长程身份保持和编辑局部性之间的权衡难题。

Method: 构建EditVid训练-free框架：1）使用稀疏因果记忆确保局部连贯性；2）通过基于对应关系的后注意力令牌注入实现长程身份保持；3）采用软潜在混合保证编辑局部性。该框架统一支持指令引导和参考引导编辑任务（风格迁移、属性修改、对象插入、部分级编辑、主体替换）。

Result: 在FiVE基准上达到78.16 FiVE-Acc（最强训练-free基线为58.95），在IVEBench上取得竞争性结果；用户研究显示对EditVid的整体偏好率为51.8%，超过7种竞争方法。

Conclusion: EditVid通过整合多种无训练技术，成功实现了统一且高质量的指令/主体引导视频编辑，验证了其在准确性和用户偏好方面的优越性，为视频编辑领域提供了高效灵活的解决方案。

Abstract: Video editing spans diverse editing paradigms, yet achieving high-quality instruction-guided and subject-guided editing within a single unified framework remains challenging. We introduce EditVid, a training-free framework combining sparse causal memory for local coherence, correspondence-based post-attention token injection for long-range identity preservation, and soft latent blending for edit locality. The same framework supports instruction-guided and reference-guided edits, including style transfer, attribute modification, object insertion, part-level editing, and subject replacement. On FiVE, EditVid achieves 78.16 FiVE-Acc, compared with 58.95 for the strongest evaluated training-free baseline, while obtaining competitive results on IVEBench. A user study further shows a 51.8\% overall preference for EditVid over 7 competing methods.

</details>


### [98] [Puffin-World: Scaling a Unified Multimodal Model with Native 3D World States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04196)
*Kang Liao,Yihang Luo,Xiao-Ming Wu,Linyi Jin,Size Wu,Chunyu Lin,Yao Zhao,Fei Wang,Wei Li,Chen Change Loy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Puffin-World，一种无需外部模块的统一多模态架构，通过联合建模物理、几何和外观状态及Omni-Camera表示，实现物理一致且视觉稳定的3D世界生成与重建，并发布了包含1500万数据点的Puffin-16M数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决现有方法依赖外部离线模块的问题，构建一个能够可靠地构建和交互于3D世界的统一框架，以支持物理理解、空间模拟及3D世界生成与重建。

Method: 提出Puffin-World架构，联合建模物理（重力场和纬度）、几何（深度）和外观（图像）三种原生世界状态，引入支持多样任务和灵活运动的统一Omni-Camera表示；通过 grounding 绝对相机属性实现物理一致性，并在单一生成过程中耦合外观与几何以同步合成视图和重建几何；构建Puffin-16M数据集进行扩展训练。

Result: 实现了物理一致且视觉稳定的3D世界生成，支持模仿和自我校准的世界探索等闭环应用；构建了包含1500万个视觉-语言-相机三元组和100万条轨迹的Puffin-16M数据集，涵盖各种具有挑战性的运动场景。

Conclusion: Puffin-World通过统一的范式实现了多任务协同，促进了3D世界生成与重建领域的发展，并通过开源代码、模型和数据集推动了进一步研究。

Abstract: We propose Puffin-World, a unified multimodal architecture that integrates physical understanding, spatial simulation, and 3D world generation and reconstruction without relying on external offline modules. To reliably construct and interact with 3D worlds, our framework jointly models three native world states: physics (gravity field and latitude), geometry (depth), and appearance (image), together with a unified Omni-Camera representation that supports diverse tasks and flexible motions. Beyond modeling these states, we introduce a strategy for propagating physical dynamics across future frames. By grounding absolute camera properties in the real world, Puffin-World enables physically consistent and visually stable world generation. We further couple appearance and geometry within a single generative process, jointly synthesizing each future view and reconstructing its underlying geometry. This unified paradigm enables interleaved closed-loop applications requiring synergy across multiple tasks, including mimic and self-calibrated world exploration. To scale Puffin-World to complex scenarios, we construct Puffin-16M, comprising 15 million vision-language-camera triplets and 1 million trajectories featuring various and challenging motions. To foster further research in this area, we released the code, models, and datasets.

</details>


### [99] [Principia: Relational Physics Tests for Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04200)
*Varun Varma Thozhiyoor,Shivam Tripathi,Venkatesh Babu Radhakrishnan,Anand Bhattad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Principia基准，通过评估视频中成对物体间的相对运动一致性来检验牛顿物理推理能力，解决了传统绝对运动测量依赖相机标定和帧率的难题。实验显示当前视频生成模型在该基准上表现极差（最高分0.42），远优于VBench评分，表明现有模型缺乏真实的物理常识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估视频模型的物理推理能力面临巨大挑战，因为绝对运动测量依赖于帧率、物体尺度和相机标定等通常模糊或不可用的信息。需要一种不依赖这些外部参数的评估方法。

Method: 引入Principia基准，涵盖重力、摩擦、动量等八种牛顿物理现象，利用受控协议下的真实场景记录。提出一种与标定无关的一致性分数，直接在图像空间中量化物理违规情况，通过比较同一场景中遵循相同物理定律的成对物体的运动关系进行评估。

Result: 在六个最先进的视频生成器生成的数千个样本中，没有任何模型在Principia上超过0.42分，尽管它们在VBench上的得分约为0.8。视觉语言模型检测物理违规的能力也较弱，最佳模型准确率仅67%，多数接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 当前主流视频生成模型和视觉语言模型在物理一致性方面存在显著缺陷，现有的通用评估指标（如VBench）无法有效反映这一短板，亟需基于相对关系的物理一致性评估标准。

Abstract: Evaluating physical reasoning in video models is difficult because absolute motion measurements depend on frame rate, object scale, and camera calibration, all of which are often ambiguous or unavailable in generated video. We propose a different approach. When two objects in the same scene obey the same physical law, their motions must satisfy predictable relationships, and these relationships hold independent of calibration. We introduce Principia, a benchmark that evaluates Newtonian physics through relational consistency between paired objects. Principia spans eight phenomena - gravity, restitution, friction, rotational inertia, projectile motion, momentum, pendulum, and mass-spring oscillation - across translational, rotational, collisional, and oscillatory dynamics, using real-world scenes recorded under controlled protocols. We also introduce a calibration-independent consistency score that quantifies physical violation directly in image space. Across thousands of generations from six state-of-the-art video generators, no model exceeds 0.42 on Principia despite all scoring around 0.8 on VBench. Vision-language models are evaluated on their ability to detect relational physics violations, with the best model achieving only 67% accuracy and most performing near chance level.

</details>


### [100] [Scal3R: Learning Efficient Multi-Relative Pose Query for Scalable Online 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04201)
*Chin-Yang Lin,Yang-Che Sun,Cheng Sun,Fu-En Yang,Min-Hung Chen,Yen-Yu Lin,Wei-Chen Chiu,Yu-Lun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Scal3R，通过冻结骨干网络并注入轻量级可学习token进行多参考系相对位姿查询，结合闭环位姿图优化，解决长视频在线3D重建中的累积漂移问题，显著降低ATE并达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线3D重建模型在处理长视频时表现不佳，因为相对于固定第一帧锚点回归位姿会导致外推超出训练分布，小漂移累积引发严重的几何坍塌。观察到尽管全局位姿失效，但逐帧深度和局部几何结构保持稳定，因此动机在于解耦局部几何与全局位姿估计。

Method: 1. 将在线重建重构为多参考系相对位姿查询任务；2. 使用约占参数1%的轻量级可学习token，通过非对称注意力机制注入完全冻结的骨干网络；3. 基于多个历史关键帧查询位姿；4. 集成带有闭环检测的在线位姿图优化系统以抑制长距离漂移。

Result: 1. 在单GPU上8小时收敛；2. KITTI数据集平均绝对轨迹误差（ATE）较在线基线降低60%以上；3. 在Virtual KITTI, Sintel, TUM-Dynamic, ScanNet, 7-Scenes等数据集上达到最先进（SOTA）性能。

Conclusion: Scal3R通过解耦局部几何特征与全局位姿估计，利用冻结骨干网络和轻量级适配器有效解决了长视频在线3D重建中的漂移问题，实现了高精度、低计算成本的实时重建。

Abstract: Online 3D reconstruction models perform poorly on long videos. This happens because regressing poses relative to a fixed first-frame anchor forces extrapolation far beyond the training distribution. Small drifts accumulate and amplify into significant geometric collapse. However, we observe that per-frame depth remains stable throughout this failure. The backbone's local geometry remains intact; only the global pose head breaks down. Motivated by this decoupling, we introduce Scal3R. This approach reformulates online reconstruction as multi-reference relative pose querying. We use lightweight learnable tokens, which make up about ~1% of the parameters, and inject them into a completely frozen backbone via asymmetric attention. This setup queries poses relative to multiple past keyframes. An online pose-graph optimization system with loop closure suppresses long-range drift. Scal3R reaches convergence in 8 hours on a single GPU. It reduces the average ATE by over 60% on KITTI compared to the online baseline. It also achieves state-of-the-art performance across Virtual KITTI, Sintel, TUM-Dynamic, ScanNet, and 7-Scenes. Project page: https://linjohnss.github.io/scal3r/

</details>


### [101] [TokenMatch: 3D Mesh Correspondence Transformer with Curvature-Guided Tokenisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04202)
*Adeela Islam,Zorah Lähner,Vittorio Murino,Vladislav Golyanik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TokenMatch，一种基于Transformer的统一模型，通过曲率引导的自适应分块和注意力机制，解决部分观测和非等距形变下的3D形状对应估计问题，无需微调即可泛化至全形状匹配，性能优越且推理速度快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的方法依赖手工特征或模板表示，而生成式功能图方法存在推理成本高、可解释性差以及对部分形状泛化能力弱的问题，难以在部分观测和强非等距形变下实现鲁棒的匹配。

Method: 引入TokenMatch，一种基于Transformer的前馈统一模型。核心创新在于利用形状曲率指导将网格自适应地标记为patch（token），并通过自注意力和交叉注意力机制高效学习patch级和点级的关系及密集对应关系。该模型仅在BeCoS数据集上训练。

Result: 在CP2P、PSMAL、BeCoS、FAUST、SCAPE和SHREC'19等多个基准测试中，TokenMatch在平均测地线误差和交并比指标上均优于现有方法，且在大多数情况下表现一致优异，推理速度达到亚秒级。

Conclusion: TokenMatch成功解决了部分和非等距形状匹配的难题，具备强大的泛化能力（无需微调即可处理全形状匹配）和高效率，为3D形状对应估计提供了新的有效解决方案。

Abstract: While data-driven 3D shape correspondence estimation has recently seen substantial progress, robust matching under partial observations and strong non-isometric deformations remains challenging. Existing learning-based approaches often rely on hand-crafted descriptors or template-based representations, whereas recent generative models over functional maps suffer from high inference cost, limited interpretability, and poor generalisation to partial shapes. In response to these limitations, this paper introduces TokenMatch, a new transformer-based unified model for estimating 3D shape correspondences. Our feed-forward approach trained exclusively on BeCoS, a challenging non-isometric partial-to-partial shape-matching dataset, can generalise to matching full shapes without retraining or fine-tuning. TokenMatch uses self- and cross-attention mechanisms to efficiently learn patch-level and point-level relations as well as dense correspondences between shape pairs. Our core insight is that meshes can be adaptively tokenised into patches using shape curvature guidance, enabling effective learning of shape-specific geometric descriptors for correspondence estimation. We evaluate TokenMatch on standard benchmarks for partial and full shape matching, including CP2P, PSMAL, BeCoS, FAUST, SCAPE, and SHREC'19. Our method achieves consistently high performance, in most cases outperforming existing methods for partial and full shape matching in the mean geodesic error and intersection-over-union metrics, while also running faster at sub-second inference speeds.

</details>


### [102] [Temporal Self-Distillation: Learning Visual State Tracking in Videos Without Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04203)
*Shravan Venkatraman,Wenshuai Zhao,Mohammad Hassan Vali,Arno Solin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出S³T框架，利用密集采样视频作为教师、稀疏采样作为学生进行自监督自蒸馏，无需标签即可提升连续视频状态追踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决连续视频状态追踪中缺乏标注数据的问题，探索如何利用时间采样密度差异实现无监督的状态学习。

Method: 构建S³T（Self-Supervised Self-Distillation over Time）框架，将同一视频片段的密集采样视图视为特权信息作为教师模型，稀疏采样视图作为学生模型；学生模型通过匹配教师模型的下一个token分布进行训练，生成自身目标，无需外部标签或奖励信号。

Result: 在LLaVA-OneVision-2-8B上，S³T使VSTAT准确率分别提升1.74（单模型）、2.38（souping融合）和2.70（结合视觉编码器适配）；该能力可迁移至真实视频，在VSTAT-YouTube状态追踪问题上提升7.95，在MVBench动作计数任务上提升4.50。

Conclusion: S³T是首个完全自包含的连续视频状态追踪框架，证明了利用时间采样密度差异进行自监督自蒸馏的有效性，且该方法无需额外推理成本即可显著提升状态追踪与动作理解能力。

Abstract: We introduce S$^3$T (Self-Supervised Self-Distillation over Time), which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first fully self-contained framework for continuous video state tracking. Our method treats temporal sampling density as privileged information, based on the hypothesis that a denser view of the same clip recovers the running state more accurately. This view serves as the teacher, while a sparse-view student with the same weights learns to match its next-token distribution. The model generates its own target, so training requires no labels, separate teacher, or reward signal, and adds no inference cost. On LLaVA-OneVision-2-8B, S$^3$T improves VSTAT accuracy by $+1.74$ as a single model, $+2.38$ with souping, and $+2.70$ with additional vision-encoder adaptation, while prior self-evolving methods leave state tracking largely unchanged. The capability learned from unlabeled synthetic clips transfers to real videos, improving performance by $+7.95$ on VSTAT-YouTube state-tracking questions and $+4.50$ on MVBench Action Count.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [Where Does Harness-Optimization Value Live? Localized Gains and the Budget-Splitting Trap in Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02889)
*Michael Nguyen,Wei Chen Tan,Nurul Aisyah Hassan,Arvind Raman,Li Hua Lim,Ahmad Faiz Razak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出HARNESSEVO方法，将LLM Agent的harness分解为四个独立槽位（角色、任务策略、工具/格式规则、反思/控制）。研究发现优化价值高度集中于“反思/控制”槽位，均匀分配预算反而有害，集中预算可显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反射式提示演化方法通常将harness作为单一扁平字符串进行优化，不清楚优化价值具体 resides 在何处，且可能存在预算分配不均导致的优化失效问题。

Method: 引入HARNESSEVO框架，将harness解耦为四个可演化槽位；结合留一法（leave-one-in/out）归因分析各槽位贡献；在不同任务（ALFWorld, WebShop）上对比不同预算分配策略的效果。

Result: 1. ALFWorld任务中，整体成功率与基线持平（0.657 vs 0.642），但归因显示全部优化价值来自“反思/控制”槽位（增益+0.119），其他槽位无效。
2. 均匀分割预算导致每个槽位低于有效搜索阈值而冻结；集中预算于高贡献槽位可将成功率提升至0.761。
3. WebShop任务中所有方法表现相同，表明该任务缺乏可 verbalizable 的控制失败模式，而非预算不足所致。

Conclusion: Harness的优化价值具有局部性（localized），均匀预算分配可能产生负面影响，结构化Agent演化应优先进行信用分配（credit assignment）。

Abstract: A growing body of work improves frozen large language models (LLMs) as agents by evolving their harness: the textual scaffolding around the model, including persona, strategy, format rules, and control heuristics. Existing reflective prompt-evolution methods usually optimize this harness as one flat string. We instead ask where the optimization value actually resides. We introduce HARNESSEVO, which decomposes the harness into four separately evolvable slots: role, task-strategy, tool/format-rules, and reflection/control. Using the same reflective optimizer under an iso-budget setting, we pair this decomposition with leave-one-in and leave-one-out attribution to measure the contribution of each slot.
  On ALFWorld with a frozen 7B backbone, HARNESSEVO does not significantly improve the overall binary success rate over either the stock harness or flat-string evolution: 0.657 versus 0.642 and 0.642, respectively. However, the slot-level analysis reveals that nearly all useful optimization value is localized in the reflection/control slot, which achieves a leave-one-in gain of +0.119. The other slots are individually null. We further show that uniform budget splitting is harmful: allocating 64 rollouts across four slots leaves only 16 per slot, below the optimizer's effective search floor, causing every slot to freeze at its empty seed. Concentrating the budget on the high-credit control slot recovers the lost gain, reaching 0.761 with half the split budget.
  The effect is task-contingent. On WebShop, all slots freeze empty and all methods tie, indicating a genuine absence of recurrent, verbalizable control failures rather than budget starvation. Overall, our results suggest that harness value is localized, uniform budget splitting can be actively harmful, and credit assignment should precede structured agent-evolution.

</details>


### [104] [Bounded Personas Match Retrieval on Classification but Not Regression for a Frozen Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02890)
*JaeHa Yoon,Minjun Park,Seoyeon Kim,Jiwoo Lee,Hyunwoo Choi,Dohyun Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PersonaLink，一种无需训练的蒸馏方法，通过递归自我评估和重写，将用户历史压缩为紧凑的三字段人格，在LaMP-2新闻分类任务上达到与检索方法相当的高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决个性化语言代理中“检索”（准确但随历史增长成本增加）与“蒸馏”（成本低、可解释但被认为牺牲准确性）两种策略之间的权衡问题，特别是验证蒸馏人格是否能匹配检索的准确性。

Method: 引入PersonaLink方法：1) 将用户历史蒸馏为有界的三字段人格；2) 递归精炼过程：使用冻结的7B骨干网络，在用户自有标注历史的保留切片上进行自评估；3) 根据错误重写人格，仅在性能不下降时保留结果；4) 通过控制上下文差异隔离表示方法与模型效应的影响。

Result: 在LaMP-2数据集（200名用户，15类新闻分类）上，PersonaLink准确率达到0.745-0.755，与BM25检索方法（0.760-0.765）在统计上无显著差异，证明了蒸馏策略在特定任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: 存在明显的任务类型不对称性，但在某些任务（如新闻分类）中，经过精心设计的蒸馏人格可以匹敌检索方法的性能，同时保持查询独立性和可解释性的优势。

Abstract: A personalized language agent must convert a user's interaction history into behavior on each new request at inference time. Two strategies dominate. Retrieval pulls a few of the user's most relevant past items into the prompt, which is accurate but pays a per-query selection and context cost that grows with the history. Distillation instead compresses the history once into a compact natural-language persona, which is bounded, query-independent, and interpretable, but is widely assumed to sacrifice accuracy. Whether, and on which tasks, a distilled persona can match retrieval has not been characterized cleanly. We introduce PersonaLink, a training-free method that distills a user's history into a bounded three-field persona and recursively refines it: each pass self-evaluates the frozen agent on a held-out slice of the user's own labeled history, rewrites the persona from its errors, and keeps the result only when it does not regress on that slice. Because every comparison shares one frozen 7B backbone and differs only in what is placed in context, the design isolates the effect of representation from that of the model. The result is a clear task-type asymmetry. On 200 users of LaMP-2 (15-way news categorization), PersonaLink reaches 0.745-0.755 accuracy, statistically indistinguishable from BM25 retrieval (0.760-0.765).

</details>


### [105] [Counterexamples as Feedback for Agent Self-Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02892)
*Sidhesh Badrinarayan,Adithya Parthasarathy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: A-CEGIS 框架利用反例作为反馈，显著提升了自然语言到正则表达式生成中的多轮修正能力，在30个任务中90%可在4轮内解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单轮代码生成指标无法反映已部署智能体在收到具体反馈后修复错误工件的能力，即缺乏对多轮迭代优化能力的评估。

Method: 提出 A-CEGIS 轻量级框架，使用确定性预言机（oracle）在全匹配语义下检查智能体生成的正则表达式，并通过紧凑的反例（假阳性或假阴性见证）作为反馈引导下一轮修正。

Result: 在30个NL-RX-Turk任务中，诊断性反例反馈在四轮预算内解决了90%的任务；相比零样本生成（17%）、通用自纠正（27%）和仅错误反馈（23%）有显著提升。在完整诊断运行中，所有隐藏集任务最终均被解决，平均成功时间为2.7轮，针对探测的稳健成功率为77%。

Conclusion: A-CEGIS 不仅衡量了智能体在多轮迭代中改进的效率，还通过超越原始保留案例的鲁棒性检查，提供了更实用的评估维度。

Abstract: Single-turn code-generation metrics understate a central property of deployed agents: whether they can repair a wrong artifact after receiving concrete feedback. This paper presents A-CEGIS, a lightweight framework that uses counterexamples as feedback for evaluating multi-turn refinement in natural-language-to-regex synthesis. An agent proposes a regex, a deterministic oracle checks it under full-match semantics, and compact false-positive or false-negative witnesses guide the next turn. On 30 NL-RX-Turk tasks, diagnostic counterexample feedback solves 90\% of tasks within a four-turn ablation budget, compared with 17% for zero-shot generation, 27% for generic self-correction, and 23% for error-only feedback. In a full diagnostic run with hardening, all tasks are solved on the hidden set by the final turn, with mean time-to-success of 2.7 turns and robust success of 77% after targeted probing. These results show that A-CEGIS measures how efficiently an agent improves across turns while adding a practical robustness check beyond the original held-out cases.

</details>


### [106] [Probe Generalization as Subspace Selection for OOD Deception Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02893)
*Daniel Yoo,Adrians Skapars*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过从训练激活分布中选择主成分（PCs）进行投影，可显著提升语言模型探针在分布外（OOD）欺骗检测任务中的泛化能力，其中由LLM判定的可迁移PC能大幅缩小性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性探针虽能检测语言模型内部行为，但在分布外示例上常失效；旨在解决Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct探针在跨域欺骗检测数据集上的泛化性能问题。

Method: 研究探针在3个保留欺骗检测数据集上的表现，通过将输入投影到训练激活分布的少量主成分（PCs）上实现跨域迁移；利用LLM评估器筛选最具迁移性的PC子集，并基于高分PC构建探针。

Result: 仅使用少量PC投影即可实现接近直接在测试分布上训练探针的性能；在Insider Trading Report和Sandbagging数据集中，使用LLM选出的高评分PC分别将基线与理想性能的差距缩小了78%和25%。

Conclusion: 源探针权重较高的方向往往编码源特定的表面特征，而真正可迁移的方向以更抽象的方式编码对比信息且能被自然语言描述捕捉；探针的OOD鲁棒性主要由子空间选择决定。

Abstract: Linear probes can be used to detect behaviors and concepts inside language model activations, but may fail to transfer to out-of-distribution examples.
